- **worker**: The Worker agent executes specific delivery tasks — producing deliverables, classifying notes, generating documents. ([Ditto190/agentic-project-management-modme](https://github.com/Ditto190/agentic-project-management-modme/blob/HEAD/delivery/_apm/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Implements exactly one work package of an approved task contract. Use from /work, one package at a time. Writes project code only inside its package's globs (the hook enforces it) and leaves a note file for the orchestrator. ([dmitrySachkov/agent-harness-template](https://github.com/dmitrySachkov/agent-harness-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Implements exactly one task issue in an assigned git worktree and reports a PR plus a summary. Started only by the build orchestrator; cannot plan stories or start other workers. ([dongqdev/haku](https://github.com/dongqdev/haku/blob/HEAD/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Runs one independent code task in parallel with others, in its own worktree, and returns a verified diff. Use only when work splits into tasks that touch disjoint files; the main session writes everything else. ([donmanueldev/momotombo-harness](https://github.com/donmanueldev/momotombo-harness/blob/HEAD/global/claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Small exact jobs - a lookup, running checks and reporting, a fact-check, bookkeeping, an exact edit. Not for building a piece or judging a design. ([doodla/midnight-village](https://github.com/doodla/midnight-village/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Execution agent spawned by the Integrator to deliver a single GitHub issue ([drdanmaggs/rocket-fuel](https://github.com/drdanmaggs/rocket-fuel/blob/HEAD/internal/plugin/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Leaf worker for one bounded, well-defined task handed out by an orchestrator. Caller picks model and effort per task. Cannot delegate. ([drecdroid/claude-base-project-agent-kit](https://github.com/drecdroid/claude-base-project-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-kit/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: General implementation worker for a focused, approved task ([dreilly/configs](https://github.com/dreilly/configs/blob/HEAD/profiles/work/pi/agents/worker.md) · 2 copies)
- **worker**: Executes ONE ticket from plans-private/tickets/&lt;NN&gt;/ and writes its drafts and proposed changes into that ticket's out/ folder. Use when the main session says a ticket is ready. Gives back a short report; never edits shared wiki files, CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md or anything outside out/. ([Dsinha97/fpl-app](https://github.com/Dsinha97/fpl-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Implements one issue of {{name}} on its own branch and opens a pull request, or fixes review findings on a PR it opened. Give it the issue or PR number. ([dubidu22/claude-project-setup-skill](https://github.com/dubidu22/claude-project-setup-skill/blob/HEAD/skills/project-setup/templates/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Per-ticket implementer. One worker owns one ticket in one worktree: reads the ticket, implements it to a draft MR, puts it up for review (a lookout fleetd spawns reviews it and posts threads; the worker answers them, round after round, until a review comes back clean), and voices its own judgment (review / concluded / escalated / anchored) to fleetd via fleetc. Runs under /goal — works until the ticket is delivered. Not a loop. ([duclos-cavalcanti/agent-factory](https://github.com/duclos-cavalcanti/agent-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/worker.md) · 2 copies)
- **worker**: One bounded implementation task ([E-Rick-M/meals-app](https://github.com/E-Rick-M/meals-app/blob/HEAD/.agents/skills/use-pi-subagents/assets/agents/worker.md) · 4 copies)
- **worker**: Implements one Solo todo - writes code, verifies it, commits with the commit skill, saves a Solo scratchpad result, and closes the todo ([EdmondFrank/pi-dist](https://github.com/EdmondFrank/pi-dist/blob/HEAD/config/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Does one trivial task on Haiku: a rename, typo, format, lookup, simple search, or other mechanical edit of about 15 lines. Use only for work the orchestrator has already classed as trivial. ([edsoncantuaria/cc-tiers](https://github.com/edsoncantuaria/cc-tiers/blob/HEAD/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Executes an assigned implementation task inside the task workspace ([EeeMt/codify](https://github.com/EeeMt/codify/blob/HEAD/deploy/worker-cli/pi-subagents/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Runs one stage of a scripted workflow that needs only files and shell — read, edit, write, grep, Bash, a background Monitor, web fetch or search — and returns exactly what the stage's prompt asks for. Use when a workflow script passes `agentType: 'worker'` on an `agent()` call (grind's default for every step but `skill`); it starts without the tool, skill and connector listings the default workflow agent carries. Not for a stage that needs Skill, Agent, ReportFindings or an MCP tool — those keep the default agent. ([ejclark/skynet-capital](https://github.com/ejclark/skynet-capital/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Task worker bee. One task, all the way, fully verified. Plan. Implement. Test. Validate. Deliver. Never refuses, never half-ships. ([eliezeraugust0/Suna---Company-Ai-Command-Center](https://github.com/eliezeraugust0/Suna---Company-Ai-Command-Center/blob/HEAD/core/kortix-master/opencode/agents/worker.md) · 2 copies)
- **worker**: General-purpose subagent with the normal Pi tools, extensions and skills, isolated context ([enzodevs/pi-core](https://github.com/enzodevs/pi-core/blob/HEAD/extensions/subagent/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Default implementation tier (Sonnet, medium effort). Use for work with a checkable result: writing or changing code against tests, a diff with a clear acceptance check, mechanical refactors, running a defined procedure. Not for review, audit or design decisions, and not for open-ended research. ([EONRaider/agent-router](https://github.com/EONRaider/agent-router/blob/HEAD/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Implements one scoped nethound issue using the project rules and layering. ([erdobozkurt/nethound](https://github.com/erdobozkurt/nethound/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Autonomous implementer on the coder role (GLM-5.3-Flash, thinking off) with full tools and an isolated context ([erewhon/dotfiles](https://github.com/erewhon/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/dot_pi/agent/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Ships exactly one step issue as one PR, in its own worktree. ([eshan06/fitted](https://github.com/eshan06/fitted/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Engineering OS worker. Runs exactly one planned step of an Engineering OS task (implement, tests, docs, design) inside the task's write scope. Started by the lead with a brief from `engos step brief`. ([eslamatwa/engineering-os](https://github.com/eslamatwa/engineering-os/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Implementation Worker for clear, unambiguous, non-complex work with objective acceptance criteria (small fixes, mechanical changes, well-specified features). Owns one goal in its assigned checkout and does not delegate. Use worker-high for ordinary bounded implementation, complex-worker for multi-step or ambiguous work. ([esnssr/machine-dots](https://github.com/esnssr/machine-dots/blob/HEAD/portable/agents/claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Default delegate for well-scoped execution steps such as implementing a planned change, refactors, running builds or scripts, and other edits no specialist covers. Not for adding tests (test-writer), read-only questions about the code (explorer), build or lint failures (build-fixer), or bugs whose cause is unclear (debugger). Use worker-high when the task clearly needs deeper reasoning. ([evictedcucumber/.claude](https://github.com/evictedcucumber/.claude/blob/HEAD/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: One scoped code slice or fix: code, targeted tests, e2e proof for user-visible changes, commit, report. Inline the brief (Ticket/Recon/State via !`cat`). UI goes to design or design-builder; small edits stay in the parent. ([FasalZein/pi-shared-config](https://github.com/FasalZein/pi-shared-config/blob/HEAD/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Execution agent for one well-defined task — mechanical edits across files, running tests or builds and reporting failures, generating boilerplate. Use only after the main session has decided exactly what to do. ([fgferre/ASTEROIDS_ROGUEFIELD](https://github.com/fgferre/ASTEROIDS_ROGUEFIELD/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Implements an exact, fully specified change. Does not redesign. ([Flexksx/config](https://github.com/Flexksx/config/blob/HEAD/dotfiles/chezmoi/dot_pi/agent/extensions/subagents/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Worker de un equipo agent-team. Ejecuta UNA tarea concreta asignada por el PI (derivar, calcular, programar, analizar datos), se auto-verifica y reporta afirmaciones precisas y verificables. Para código, los tests van en el mismo cambio. ([FlorJDC/agent-team-template](https://github.com/FlorJDC/agent-team-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Default subagent for the orchestrate skill. Investigates or implements one scoped piece of work from a written brief and reports the result. ([FluroAsh/claude-skills](https://github.com/FluroAsh/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/ash-skills/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Integrated worker that advances implementation, preflight self-check, verification, and commit prep per task ([foden303/harness](https://github.com/foden303/harness/blob/HEAD/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Implements exactly one slice of an approved plan with TDD inside the allowed files and returns a handoff with evidence. Use for each implementation slice dispatched by the delivering-changes skill, never for planning or review. ([galileo-studio-dev/skills](https://github.com/galileo-studio-dev/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery/agents/worker.md))
